# Exhaustive file audit

Audit dates: 23–26 October 2026 (Australia/Sydney). Baseline commit:
`678b48325189f83beb942bc6759e7c519eb26b19`.

The baseline contains **1,453 tracked files**. Every path is accounted for exactly
once: **550 applicable human-maintained files reviewed**, including authored
fields in mixed research artifacts; **903 generated, imported or binary files
explicitly excluded from manual source review and individually validated**.
There are no missing or duplicate baseline paths. Ten new implementation/test
files and the six audit documents are also listed: **1,469 rows total**.

Code, configuration, maintained prose and tests were read in full. Structured
curation and historical decision records were inspected as data; repeated
identical authored values were read once with every source-file mapping retained.
In mixed artifacts, generated prompts, copied third-party guide text and hash
inventories were parsed and checked separately. A reviewed historical claim is
not a fresh verification of that claim, artwork technique or production service.

Binary review means the checks named in each row (complete image decode, video
probe, font/public-key parsing, dimensions, hashes or references), not manual
inspection of every historical pixel/frame. Current running-page screenshots
are covered separately in [regression tests](REGRESSION_TESTS.md).
Package caches, build output, `.git`, browser traces and audit scratch files are
untracked tool outputs outside this source inventory; their controlling files
are included. No applicable source path was omitted based on its extension.

Verification wording distinguishes source review from executed tests. The final
shared suite outcomes and any failures are authoritative in
[REGRESSION_TESTS.md](REGRESSION_TESTS.md), rather than inferred from a clean row.
